379:
380:def preflight(cfg, repeat=False):
381:    """Before launch, native and Debian: ports, agents, files/hashes, no shadowing libs, not already done,
382:    plus Debian env/imports or native root/battery/cpuset."""
383:    v1.free_ports()
384:    matches = v1.agent_matches()
385:    if matches:
386:        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
387:        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
388:    missing = [str(p) for p in files_of(cfg) if not p.is_file()]
389:    if missing or not libs_of(BUILDS[cfg['build']]):
390:        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing or ['build shared libraries']))
391:    # Both launchers list $PREFIX/lib before their own build dir in RUNPATH: a packaged libllama would shadow both.
392:    shadow = sorted(p.name for p in (v1.NATIVE.parent/'usr/lib').glob('lib*') if re.match(r'lib(ggml|llama|mtmd)', p.name))
393:    if shadow:
394:        raise SystemExit('Termux $PREFIX/lib would shadow the build libraries: ' + ', '.join(shadow))
395:    freq_state()
396:    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():  # Debian: the side that builds payloads and runs the servers
397:        check_env()
398:        needed = ['requests'] + (['PIL'] if cfg['test'] == 'vision' else [])
399:        if [m for m in needed if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]:
400:            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(needed) + ' — run: apt-get install python3-requests python3-pil')
401:        spec = manifest(cfg)
402:        bad = [p for p in weights_of(cfg) if spec['weight_sha256'][str(p)] != WEIGHTS[p]]
403:        if bad:
404:            raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(map(str, bad)))
405:        if not repeat and (prior := done_before(spec)):
406:            raise SystemExit(f'already measured VALID with identical code and configuration: {prior} (--repeat to redo)')
407:    else:
408:        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
409:            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
410:        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
411:        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
412:            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
413:        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
414:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
415:        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
416:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
417:        if not stuck:
418:            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
419:    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
420:
421:
422:def med(xs):
423:    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
424:    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None
425:
426:
427:def summary(folder):
428:    """One result, from saved files only; missing fields are None (unavailable)."""
429:    d = json.loads((Path(folder)/'result.json').read_text())
430:    r, m = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {})
431:    by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
432:    dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in r)
433:    start = d.get('measure_start_snapshot') or d.get('start') or {}
434:    return dict(
435:        folder=str(folder), test=d['test'], build=d['build'], mtp=d['mtp'], q8=d['q8'], label=d['label'],
436:        reason=d['reason'], load_s=med([d.get('load_s')]), markers=d.get('markers'),
437:        first_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('first')]),
438:        uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
439:        cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
440:        gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
441:        draft_acceptance=round(sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in r)/dn, 3) if dn else None,
442:        image_wall_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('image')]),
443:        image_tokens=sorted({x.get('image_tokens') for x in by('image')}, key=str),
444:        prompt_n={x['input']: x['prompt_tokens'] for x in r}, gen_tokens={x['input']: x['generated_tokens'] for x in r},
445:        checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
446:                    cached_reuse=all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
447:                    replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r),
448:                    libs_ok=(d.get('libs') or {}).get('ok')),
449:        pss_after_load_mib=(d.get('pss_after_load') or {}).get('pss_kib') and round(d['pss_after_load']['pss_kib']/1024),
450:        peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
451:        min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
452:        mean_battery_w=(m.get('power') or {}).get('mean_battery_w'),
453:        start=dict(skin=start.get('skin'), status=start.get('status'), bat_c=start.get('bat_c'),
454:                   capacity=start.get('capacity'), max=start.get('max')),
455:        skin_end=(d.get('end') or {}).get('skin'), status_max=m.get('status_max'),
456:        caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()},
457:        freq=d.get('freq'), lmk=d.get('lmk'), exit_clean=d.get('exit_clean'))
458:
459:
460:def conditions(a, b):
461:    """Whether two results ran under matched operating conditions; each criterion reported separately."""
462:    sa, sb = a['start'], b['start']
463:    known = lambda *xs: all(x is not None for x in xs)
464:    c = dict(
465:        same_test=a['test'] == b['test'],
466:        both_valid=a['label'] == b['label'] == 'VALID',
467:        start_skin_within=known(sa['skin'], sb['skin']) and abs(sa['skin']-sb['skin']) <= MATCH_SKIN_C,
468:        start_status_normal=sa['status'] == sb['status'] == 0,
469:        battery_within=known(sa['capacity'], sb['capacity']) and abs(sa['capacity']-sb['capacity']) <= MATCH_BATTERY_PCT,
470:        start_caps_equal=bool(sa['max'] and sb['max']) and all(sa['max'].get(p) == sb['max'].get(p) for p in POLICIES))
471:    fa, fb = (x['freq'] or {} for x in (a, b))
472:    mhz = {p: [(f.get(p) or {}).get('mean_mhz') for f in (fa, fb)] for p in POLICIES}
473:    measured = all(known(*v) and abs(v[0]-v[1]) <= MATCH_FREQ_REL*max(v) for v in mhz.values())
474:    return dict(c, matched=all(c.values()), start_skin=[sa['skin'], sb['skin']], measured_mean_mhz=mhz,
475:                measured_freq_within=measured,
476:                note='matched = start conditions only; measured frequency also reflects each build\'s own load')
477:
478:
479:def comparable(ma, mb):
480:    """Whether two runs measured the same thing; only the build (binary path and its hashes) may differ."""
481:    same_but_build = lambda c: {k: v for k, v in c.items() if k != 'build'}
482:    ca, cb = ma['command'], mb['command']
483:    c = dict(same_configuration=same_but_build(ma['config']) == same_but_build(mb['config']),
484:             same_command_except_binary=len(ca) == len(cb) and ca[:7] + ca[8:] == cb[:7] + cb[8:],
485:             same_payloads=ma['payload_sha256'] == mb['payload_sha256'],
486:             same_weights=ma['weight_sha256'] == mb['weight_sha256'],
487:             same_harness_code=ma['code_sha256'] == mb['code_sha256'],
488:             same_constants=ma['constants'] == mb['constants'])
489:    return dict(c, comparable=all(c.values()), builds=[ma['config']['build'], mb['config']['build']])
490:
491:
492:def report(*folders):
493:    lines = [summary(f) for f in folders]
494:    for line in lines:
495:        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
496:    if len(lines) == 2:
497:        a, b = lines
498:        num = lambda x: isinstance(x, (int, float)) and not isinstance(x, bool)
499:        ratio = {k: round(b[k]/a[k], 3) for k in a if num(a[k]) and num(b[k]) and a[k]}
500:        ma, mb = (json.loads((Path(f)/'run.json').read_text())['manifest'] for f in folders)
501:        print(json.dumps(dict(second_over_first=ratio, configuration=comparable(ma, mb), conditions=conditions(a, b),
502:                              prompt_n_identical=a['prompt_n'] == b['prompt_n'],
503:                              gen_tokens_identical=a['gen_tokens'] == b['gen_tokens'])), flush=True)
504:    return lines
505:
506:
507:def parse(argv):
508:    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
509:    ap.add_argument('--test', choices=TESTS)
510:    ap.add_argument('--build', choices=tuple(BUILDS))
511:    ap.add_argument('--mtp', action='store_true', help='load the MTP drafter (implied by --test mtp)')
512:    ap.add_argument('--q8', action='store_true', help='load the Q8 projector, new build only (implied by --test vision)')
513:    ap.add_argument('--repeat', action='store_true', help='measure again although an identical VALID result exists')
514:    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
515:    ap.add_argument('--report', type=Path, nargs='+', help='print one result, or two plus their comparison')
516:    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
517:    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
518:    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
519:    ap.add_argument('--name', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
520:    return ap.parse_args(argv)
521:
522:
523:def main(argv=None):
524:    a = parse(argv)
525:    if a.monitor:
526:        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
527:    if a.report:
528:        return report(*a.report)
529:    if not (a.test and a.build):
530:        raise SystemExit('--test and --build are required')
531:    try:
532:        cfg = config(a.test, a.build, a.mtp, a.q8)
533:    except ValueError as e:
534:        raise SystemExit(str(e))
535:    if a.name:
536:        return print(suffix(cfg, a.repeat))
537:    if a.preflight:
538:        return preflight(cfg, a.repeat)
539:    if a.dry_run:
540:        warm, seq = payloads(cfg)
541:        print(DRY_LABEL)
542:        print(json.dumps(dict(config=cfg, command=command(cfg), warmup=warm and warm[1],
543:                              sequence=[(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in seq]), indent=1))
544:        return
545:    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
546:        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis3/oneshot.sh into Debian')
547:    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
548:    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
549:        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
550:    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
551:        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
552:    check_env()
553:    v1.free_ports()
554:    v2.telemetry(bridge)
555:    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
556:    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
557:    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
558:        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
559:    spec = manifest(cfg)
560:    if any(spec['weight_sha256'][str(p)] != WEIGHTS[p] for p in weights_of(cfg)):
561:        raise SystemExit('model/drafter/projector SHA-256 mismatch')
562:    if not a.repeat and (prior := done_before(spec)):
563:        raise SystemExit(f'already measured VALID: {prior} (--repeat to redo)')
564:    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=spec, repeat=a.repeat,
565:             note='One independent measurement; no thermal-equivalence gate. Compare with --report A B, '
566:                  'which states whether start conditions matched.'))
567:    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
568:    result = run_test(cfg, out, bridge)
569:    print(f"{cfg['test']} {cfg['build']}{' mtp' if cfg['mtp'] else ''}{' q8' if cfg['q8'] else ''}: "
570:          f"{result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
571:    report(out)
572:    if result['label'] != 'VALID':
573:        raise SystemExit(1)
574:
575:
576:if __name__ == '__main__':
577:    try:
578:        main()
579:    finally:
580:        for proc in v1.OWNED:
581:            v1.own_stop(proc)

+"""VIS-3B: one independent measurement per launch, b1609-dotprod (old) or b11716 (new); owner-only, motors off.
+
+Reuses VIS-2 (vis2_run.py, imported unchanged) and through it VIS-1. --dry-run is NOT VALID TIMING.
+"""
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import importlib.util
+import json
+import os
+from pathlib import Path
+import re
+import signal
+import statistics
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import time
+
+sys.dont_write_bytecode = True
+HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
+REPO = HERE.parents[2]
+_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('vis2_run', REPO/'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/vis2_run.py')
+v2 = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
+_spec.loader.exec_module(v2)
+v1 = v2.v1
+
+BUILDS = dict(old='b1609-dotprod', new='vis')
+TESTS = ('cold_load', 'warm_load', 'first_response', 'prompt', 'gen', 'mtp', 'vision')
+LOAD_TESTS = ('cold_load', 'warm_load')
+WEIGHTS = {p: v1.EXPECTED_HASHES[p] for p in (v1.MODEL, v1.DRAFT, v1.Q8)}
+ROUNDS = 4            # prompt: 4 x (uncached, cached); vision: 4 frames; gen/mtp: the 6 VIS-2 prompts
+FIRST_N = 16          # first-response request: gen[0] prompt, 16 tokens
+IMAGE_TOKENS = 70     # Q8 projector budget, as VIS-2
+VISION_FRAMES = v1.FRAMES[:ROUNDS]
+WARMUP_FRAME = v1.FRAMES[ROUNDS]
+TEST_CAP_S = 600
+REQ_TIMEOUT_S = 120
+LOAD_TIMEOUT_S = 180
+# Cross-run comparison: start conditions count as matched within these limits (reported, never enforced).
+MATCH_SKIN_C = 1.0
+MATCH_BATTERY_PCT = 10
+MATCH_FREQ_REL = .05
+POLICIES = ('policy4', 'policy6')  # cores 4-5 (A78) and 6-7 (X1) = taskset 4-7
+CPUFREQ = Path('/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpufreq')
+DRY_LABEL = v2.DRY_LABEL
+# First occurrence of each line in the default-verbosity server log; both builds print all of them.
+MARKERS = dict(load_start='load_model: loading model', vocab="control-looking token", kv_cache='llama_kv_cache_iswa:',
+               draft_start='common_speculative_init_result: loading draft model', loaded='llama_server: model loaded')
+STAMP = re.compile(r'^(\d+)\.(\d\d)\.(\d{3})\.(\d{3}) ')
+LIB = re.compile(r'(/\S*/lib(?:ggml|llama|mtmd)[^/\s]*\.so[.\d]*)$')
+ENV_OVERRIDE = re.compile(r'^(LLAMA_|GGML_|OMP_|KMP_|LD_LIBRARY_PATH$|LD_PRELOAD$)')
+
+
+def config(test, build, mtp=False, q8=False):
+    """mtp = 'gen' with the drafter; vision needs the projector, which only the new build serves."""
+    if test not in TESTS or build not in BUILDS:
+        raise ValueError(f'--test one of {TESTS}, --build old|new')
+    if test == 'gen' and mtp:
+        raise ValueError('generation with the drafter is --test mtp')
+    q8 = q8 or test == 'vision'
+    if q8 and build == 'old':
+        raise ValueError('the Q8 projector is measured on the new build only')
+    return dict(test=test, build=build, mtp=mtp or test == 'mtp', q8=q8)
+
+
+def suffix(cfg, repeat=False):
+    """Run-folder label from the validated configuration, independent of argument spelling or order."""
+    return (f"_{cfg['test']}_{cfg['build']}" + ('_mtp' if cfg['mtp'] and cfg['test'] != 'mtp' else '')
+            + ('_q8' if cfg['q8'] and cfg['test'] != 'vision' else '') + ('_repeat' if repeat else ''))
+
+
+def command(cfg):
+    """server_manager.start_setup('setup_q4') flags (ctx 2048), plus taskset/timeout/port; drafter/projector as asked."""
+    cmd = ['taskset', '-c', '4-7', 'timeout', '--signal=TERM', '--kill-after=3', '900', str(v1.BINS[BUILDS[cfg['build']]]),
+           '-m', str(v1.MODEL), '--port', '8091', '--ctx-size', '2048', '--threads', '4', '--threads-batch', '4',
+           '--parallel', '1', '--swa-full', '--cache-ram', '0', '--host', '127.0.0.1']
+    if cfg['mtp']:
+        cmd += v1.DRAFT_ARGS
+    if cfg['q8']:
+        cmd += ['--mmproj', str(v1.Q8), '--image-min-tokens', str(IMAGE_TOKENS), '--image-max-tokens', str(IMAGE_TOKENS)]
+    return cmd
+
+
+def weights_of(cfg):
+    return [v1.MODEL] + ([v1.DRAFT] if cfg['mtp'] else []) + ([v1.Q8] if cfg['q8'] else [])
+
+
+def binaries_of(cfg):
+    return [v1.BINS[BUILDS[cfg['build']]]] + libs_of(BUILDS[cfg['build']])
+
+
+def files_of(cfg):
+    """Everything the server reads at load: weights it uses plus its build's launcher and libraries."""
+    return weights_of(cfg) + binaries_of(cfg)
+

benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:68:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:69:Expected time per launch: about 10 s for the monitor to start, then load 5–10 s and priming a few seconds.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:70:Measurement: load tests about 10 s, `first_response` about 3 s, `prompt` about 3 min, `gen`/`mtp` 1–2 min, `vision`
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:71:about 1 min. For comparable pairs, run old and new back to back and alternate their order when repeating.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:72:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:73:Folders: `vis3/runs/<UTC>_<test>_<build>[_mtp][_q8][_repeat]/`, named from the validated configuration whatever the
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:74:argument spelling or order. Each holds `run.json` (configuration, command, payload and code hashes, hashes of the weights and the one
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:75:build this test uses, base commit, git status), `result.json`, `server.log`, `samples.jsonl`, `console.log`.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:76:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:77:`--report A B` prints both results and B/A ratios, then two separate assessments. `configuration` (from both
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:78:`run.json` manifests) says whether the runs measured the same thing. Only the build may differ; it checks the same test and
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:79:`--mtp`/`--q8`, the same command apart from the binary, and the same payloads, weights, harness code and constants.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:80:`conditions` says whether the two ran under matched operating conditions:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:81:- same test, both VALID;
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:82:- start skin within 1.0 °C, both at Android status 0;
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:83:- battery within 10 %;
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:84:- equal policy4/6 caps at the start.
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:85:
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:86:It also reports separately whether the measured mean frequencies are within 5 % (informational: they also reflect each
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:87:build's own load), and whether prompt token counts and generated lengths are identical. Unmatched pairs are labelled as
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:88:such, not discarded. Short tests do not establish thermal equilibrium (#93). These are Termux prototype results, not
benchmark/gemma_vision/vis3/README.md:89:the Android app.

20:@contextlib.contextmanager
21:def patched(*triples):
22:    saved = [(obj, name, getattr(obj, name)) for obj, name, _ in triples]
23:    try:
24:        for obj, name, value in triples:
25:            setattr(obj, name, value)
26:        yield
27:    finally:
28:        for obj, name, value in saved:
29:            setattr(obj, name, value)

32:def deployed_command(setup):
33:    """The exact shell command server_manager.start_setup builds; os.system and waits are stubbed."""
34:    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
35:    import server_manager as sm
36:    calls = []
37:    with patched((sm.os, 'system', calls.append), (sm.time, 'sleep', lambda s: None),
38:                 (sm, 'wait_for_server', lambda port=8080, timeout=60: True), (sm, 'mtp_draft_ok', lambda: True)):
39:        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
40:            sm.start_setup(setup)
41:    return shlex.split(calls[-1].replace(' 2>/dev/null &', ''))

101:def test_freq():
102:    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
103:        root = Path(tmp)
104:        for p, rows in (('policy4', '500000 10\n2348000 30\n'), ('policy6', '500000 0\n2850000 5\n')):
105:            (root/p/'stats').mkdir(parents=True)
106:            (root/p/'stats/time_in_state').write_text(rows)
107:        a = v.freq_state(root)
108:        (root/'policy4/stats/time_in_state').write_text('500000 10\n2348000 40\n')
109:        (root/'policy6/stats/time_in_state').write_text('500000 10\n2850000 15\n')
110:        d = v.freq_delta(a, v.freq_state(root))
111:    assert d['policy4'] == dict(ticks=10, mean_mhz=2348, top_share=1.0)
112:    assert d['policy6'] == dict(ticks=20, mean_mhz=1675, top_share=.5)
113:    assert v.freq_delta(a, a)['policy6']['mean_mhz'] is None

177:                max=dict(policy4=2348000, policy6=2850000), t=1.)

180:        live.update(proc=Proc(), pid=99)

182:        return 1.5

184:    def timed(bridge, pid, logpath, kind, name, endpoint, body, timeout):
185:        return dict(kind=kind, input=name, wall_ms=100., prompt_ms=50., prompt_tokens=10, generated_tokens=5,
186:                    predicted_per_second=10., prompt_per_second=200., cache_n=0 if kind != 'cached' else 9,
187:                    reply='ok', draft_n=None, draft_n_accepted=None, freq=None)

191:            raise RuntimeError('configuration mismatch')

193:    freq = dict(policy4={2348000: 10}, policy6={2850000: 10})

195:                 (v, 'mapped_libs', lambda pid, b: dict(libs=['/elsewhere/libllama.so'], ok=libs_ok)),

197:                 (v, 'prime', lambda paths: calls.__setitem__('prime', calls['prime'] + 1) or dict(bytes=1, s=0)),
198:                 (v, 'freq_state', lambda root=None: freq),
199:                 (v2, 'telemetry', lambda b: tele), (v2, 'fresh_pss', lambda b, p: dict(pss_kib=2048)),
200:                 (v2, 'guarded_post', lambda b, p, e, body, t: calls['posts'].append(e) or {}),
201:                 (v1, 'check_cores', lambda pid: None), (v1, 'agent_found', lambda log=False: False),
202:                 (v1, 'own_stop', lambda proc, pid=None: calls['stops'].append(pid) or 0),

204:                 (v1, 'metrics', lambda r: {})):

207:        target = json.loads((out/'target.json').read_text())

300:def manifest_of(rec, **over):

302:    return dict(dict(config=cfg, command=v.command(cfg), payload_sha256={'A5': 'p'}, weight_sha256={'m': 'w'},

306:def report_of(a, b, ma=None, mb=None):
307:    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
308:        for name, rec, man in (('a', a, ma), ('b', b, mb)):
309:            Path(tmp, name).mkdir()
310:            Path(tmp, name, 'result.json').write_text(json.dumps(rec))
311:            Path(tmp, name, 'run.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(manifest=man or manifest_of(rec))))
312:        out = io.StringIO()
313:        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
314:            lines = v.report(Path(tmp, 'a'), Path(tmp, 'b'))
315:    return lines, json.loads(out.getvalue().splitlines()[-1])

364:def test_folder_suffix_is_normalized():
365:    def name(*argv):
366:        out = io.StringIO()
367:        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
368:            v.main(['--name', *argv])
369:        return out.getvalue().strip()
370:    assert name('--test', 'gen', '--build', 'new') == name('--build=new', '--test=gen') == '_gen_new'
371:    assert name('--test', 'cold_load', '--test', 'gen', '--build', 'old') == '_gen_old', 'last option wins, as argparse'
372:    assert name('--test', 'mtp', '--build', 'old') == '_mtp_old' and name('--test', 'vision', '--build', 'new') == '_vision_new'
373:    assert name('--test', 'prompt', '--build', 'new', '--q8', '--mtp', '--repeat') == '_prompt_new_mtp_q8_repeat'
374:    for bad in (['--test', 'vision', '--build', 'old'], ['--test', 'gen']):
375:        try:

88:def sequence(block, smoke, gen, unc, cached, image=None):
89:    """(kind, name, endpoint, body). Fixed order; identical between matched configs."""
90:    if block == 'M':
91:        g = gen[0]
92:        seq = [('gen', *g), ('uncached', *unc), ('image', IMAGE_FRAME, image), ('gen', *g), ('uncached', *unc)]
93:    else:
94:        seq = []
95:        for k in range(1 if smoke else ROUNDS):
96:            seq += [('uncached', *unc), ('cached', *cached[k]), ('gen', *gen[k])]
97:    return [(kind, name, '/v1/chat/completions' if kind == 'image' else '/completion', body)
98:            for kind, name, body in seq]

101:def get(path):
102:    with urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8091'+path, timeout=10) as r:
103:        return json.load(r)

106:def verify_config(block):
107:    """The running server must report the intended projector and MTP state; never adjust flags."""
108:    c = config_of(block)
109:    props, slots = get('/props'), get('/slots')
110:    vision = props.get('modalities', {}).get('vision')
111:    spec = [s.get('speculative') for s in slots]
112:    got = dict(vision=vision, speculative=spec)
113:    if vision is not c['q8'] or spec != [c['mtp']]:
114:        raise RuntimeError(f'configuration mismatch: expected q8={c["q8"]} mtp={c["mtp"]}, server reports {got}')
115:    return got

118:def fresh_pss(bridge, pid, wait=5):
119:    """PSS of this server from a monitor sample begun after now, taken while the server is idle."""
120:    after, deadline = time.monotonic(), time.monotonic() + wait
121:    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
122:        for s in reversed(v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')):
123:            if s.get('t_start', -1) <= after:
124:                break
125:            if s.get('pid') == pid:
126:                return dict(pss_kib=s.get('pss_kib'), t=s['t'], after=after)
127:        time.sleep(.2)
128:    return dict(pss_kib=None, t=None, after=after, unavailable=f'no fresh sample within {wait} s')

130:
131:def telemetry(bridge):
132:    """Marked copy of vis1_run.telemetry (VIS2-BATTERY-FIX): no 80 % upper bound; all other stops kept."""
133:    if (bridge/'abort').exists():
134:        raise SystemExit('owner aborted wrapper')
135:    d = json.loads((bridge/'latest.json').read_text())
136:    if time.monotonic() - d['t'] > 12:
137:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry stale')
138:    if d.get('error'):
139:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry failed: ' + d['error'])
140:    if d['capacity'] < 50 or d['battery_status'] != 'Discharging':
141:        raise RuntimeError('battery below 50% or not Discharging')
142:    if d['agent']:
143:        v1.log_agent_matches(d.get('agent_detail', []))
144:        raise RuntimeError('agent found')
145:    if d['skin'] is None or d['status'] is None:
146:        raise RuntimeError('missing VIRTUAL-SKIN or Android status')
147:    if d['status'] >= 4 or d['bat_c'] >= 45:
148:        raise RuntimeError('thermal stop (Android CRITICAL or battery >=45 C)')
149:    return d

151:
152:def gate(bridge, idle):
153:    """Marked copy of vis1_run.gate using the local telemetry()."""
154:    start = time.monotonic()
155:    while True:
156:        d = telemetry(bridge)
157:        if v1.gate_ok(d['skin'], idle):
158:            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=time.monotonic()-start, missed=False)
159:        if time.monotonic()-start >= 900:
160:            raise RuntimeError('gate missed: not cool after 15 minutes; no warm-start block')
161:        time.sleep(5)
162:
163:
164:def guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, timeout):
165:    """Request in a worker; telemetry/abort/affinity stay enforced every 0.5 s while it runs.
166:    A violation raises here and run_block's finally stops the owned server, ending the request."""
167:    result = {}
168:
169:    def work():
170:        try:
171:            result['data'] = v1.post(endpoint, body, timeout=timeout)
172:        except BaseException as e:
173:            result['error'] = e
174:    th = threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True)
175:    th.start()
176:    while th.is_alive():
177:        th.join(.5)
178:        if th.is_alive():
179:            telemetry(bridge)
180:            v1.check_cores(pid)
181:    if 'error' in result:
182:        raise result['error']
183:    return result['data']
184:
185:
186:def sample_violations(samples):
187:    """Every recorded sample must satisfy the same stops telemetry() enforces live."""
188:    bad = []
189:    if any(s.get('agent') for s in samples):
190:        bad.append('agent')
191:    if any(s.get('battery_status') != 'Discharging' or s.get('capacity', -1) < 50 for s in samples):
192:        bad.append('battery band')
193:    if any(s.get('cores_ok') is False for s in samples):
194:        bad.append('affinity')
195:    if any((s.get('status') or 0) >= 4 or (s.get('bat_c') or 0) >= 45 for s in samples):
196:        bad.append('thermal stop threshold')
197:    return bad
198:
199:
200:def run_block(block, index, out, bridge, idle, seq, smoke):
201:    record = dict(phase_start=time.monotonic(), block=block, index=index, config=config_of(block),
202:                  label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[],
203:                  command=command(block), started_epoch=time.time(), smoke=smoke)
204:    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
205:    path = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.json'
206:    logpath = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
207:    proc = pid = None
208:    try:
209:        if not smoke:
210:            record['gate'] = gate(bridge, idle)
211:        v1.free_ports()
212:        if v1.agent_found(log=True):
213:            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
214:        if not smoke:  # cold load: same native cache-drop handshake as VIS-1
215:            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
216:            (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
217:            began = time.monotonic()
218:            while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
219:                if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
220:                    raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
221:                time.sleep(.1)
222:            if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
223:                raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
224:            (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
225:            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
226:            record['gate_after_drop'] = gate(bridge, idle)
227:        d = telemetry(bridge)
228:        record['battery_start'] = dict(capacity=d['capacity'], voltage_now_uV=d['voltage_now_uV'], t=d['t'])
229:        with logpath.open('w') as log:
230:            began = time.monotonic()
231:            proc = v1.spawn(record['command'], log)
232:            while pid is None:
233:                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
234:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
235:                try:
236:                    pid = v1.server_pid(proc)
237:                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
238:                    time.sleep(.05)
239:            v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=pid))
240:            while True:
241:                if proc.poll() is not None:
242:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
243:                try:
244:                    if v1.post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
245:                        break
246:                except (OSError, ValueError):
247:                    pass
248:                telemetry(bridge)
249:                if time.monotonic()-began >= 180:
250:                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
251:                time.sleep(.2)
252:            record.update(server_pid=pid, load_s=time.monotonic()-began, loaded_at=time.monotonic())
253:            v1.check_cores(pid)
254:            record['reported'] = verify_config(block)
255:            record['pss_after_load'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
256:            warm = next(x for x in seq if x[0] == 'gen')
257:            guarded_post(bridge, pid, warm[2], warm[3], REQ_TIMEOUT_S)  # discarded warm-up (generation)
258:            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
259:            for kind, name, endpoint, body in seq:
260:                telemetry(bridge)
261:                v1.check_cores(pid)
262:                left = BLOCK_CAP_S - (time.monotonic()-start)
263:                if left <= 0:
264:                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
265:                if kind == 'image':
266:                    record['pss_before_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
267:                offset = logpath.stat().st_size
268:                t = time.monotonic()
269:                data = guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, min(REQ_TIMEOUT_S, left))
270:                end = time.monotonic()
271:                if end-start > BLOCK_CAP_S:
272:                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
273:                if kind == 'image':
274:                    record['pss_after_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
275:                with logpath.open() as f:
276:                    f.seek(offset)
277:                    delta = f.read()
278:                row = v1.request_record(name, data, t, end, delta, 'V2' if kind == 'image' else 'T2')
279:                row.update(kind=kind, cache_n=row['timings'].get('cache_n'),
280:                           prompt_per_second=row['timings'].get('prompt_per_second'))
281:                record['requests'].append(row)
282:                v1.write(path, record)
283:            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
284:            if proc.poll() is not None:
285:                raise RuntimeError('server crashed during measurement')
286:            v1.check_cores(pid)
287:            telemetry(bridge)
288:            record.update(label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'VALID', reason='completed fixed request sequence')
289:    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
290:        record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason=str(e))
291:    finally:
292:        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()
293:        v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
294:        if proc is not None:
295:            record['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
296:            record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not v1.group_alive(proc.pid)
297:        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
298:        record['samples'] = [s for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
299:                             if record['phase_start']-1 <= s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
300:        record['lmk'] = v1.lmk_request(bridge, record['started_epoch'])
301:        record['metrics'] = v1.metrics(record)
302:        if record['label'] != 'NOT VALID':  # VALID and SMOKE both need a clean finish
303:            bad = []
304:            if proc is not None and not record['exit_clean']:
305:                bad.append(f"server cleanup not clean (exit {record['server_exit']})")
306:            if not record['lmk'].get('ok'):
307:                bad.append('LMK query failed')
308:            if sample_violations(record['samples']):
309:                bad.append('sample violation: ' + ', '.join(sample_violations(record['samples'])))
310:            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
311:                bad.append('sampling failed')
312:            if bad:
313:                record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason='; '.join(bad))
314:        v1.write(path, record)
315:    return record

318:def blocks(arg):
319:    sel = arg.split(',')
320:    if not sel or any(b not in BLOCKS for b in sel):
321:        raise ValueError('--blocks takes a comma list of A,B,C,D,M (repeats allowed, e.g. A,B,B,A)')
322:    return sel

325:def code_paths():
326:    return ['benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/vis2_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis2/oneshot.sh',
327:            'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis1/vis1_run.py', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py',
328:            'benchmark/llm_objective_setting/bench.py', 'benchmark/campaign/power.py',
329:            'benchmark/campaign/runtime.py', 'chat.py', 'server_manager.py']

331:
332:def manifest(selected, smoke, gen, unc, cached, image):
333:    allp = gen + [unc] + cached + ([(IMAGE_FRAME, image)] if image else [])
334:    return dict(blocks=selected, smoke=smoke, rounds=ROUNDS, gen_n=GEN_N, block_cap_s=BLOCK_CAP_S,
335:                commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
336:                payload_sha256={n: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(p, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest() for n, p in allp},
337:                code_sha256={p: v1.sha(REPO/p) for p in code_paths()},
338:                weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
339:                binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in [BIN] + LIBS},
340:                base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip(),
341:                git_status=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain=v1'], cwd=REPO, text=True))
342:
343:
344:def preflight():
345:    """Before and after the 5-minute idle: ports, agents, files/hashes, plus Debian imports or native root/battery/cpuset."""
346:    v1.free_ports()
347:    matches = v1.agent_matches()
348:    if matches:
349:        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
350:        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
351:    missing = [str(p) for p in (*WEIGHTS, BIN) if not p.is_file()]
352:    if missing or not LIBS:
353:        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing or ['llama.cpp-vis shared libraries']))
354:    bad = [str(p) for p, h in WEIGHTS.items() if v1.sha(p) != h]
355:    if bad:
356:        raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(bad))
357:    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
358:        missing_mods = [m for m in v1.DEBIAN_IMPORTS if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]
359:        if missing_mods:
360:            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(missing_mods))
361:    else:
362:        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
363:            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
364:        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
365:        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
366:            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
367:        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
368:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
369:        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
370:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
371:        if not stuck:
372:            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
373:    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)
374:
375:
376:def mib(snap):
377:    return snap['pss_kib'] and round(snap['pss_kib']/1024) if snap else None
378:
379:
380:def med(xs):
381:    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
382:    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None
383:
384:
385:def summarize(folder):
386:    """Per-block medians from saved records only; missing fields print as None (unavailable)."""
387:    lines = []
388:    for p in sorted(Path(folder).glob('[0-9][0-9]_[A-DM]_*Z.json')):
389:        d = json.loads(p.read_text())
390:        r, m = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {})
391:        by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
392:        dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
393:        da = sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
394:        lines.append(dict(
395:            block=f"{d['index']:02d}_{d['block']}", label=d['label'], reason=d['reason'], load_s=med([d.get('load_s')]),
396:            battery_start=d.get('battery_start'),
397:            reported=d.get('reported'), uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
398:            uncached_prompt_n=sorted({x['prompt_tokens'] for x in by('uncached')}),
399:            cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
400:            cached_cache_n=sorted({x['cache_n'] for x in by('cached')}),
401:            gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
402:            gen_tokens=[x['generated_tokens'] for x in by('gen')],
403:            gen_wall_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('gen')]),
404:            draft_acceptance=round(da/dn, 3) if dn else None,
405:            image=[dict(wall_ms=round(x['wall_ms']), prompt_tokens=x['prompt_tokens'], usage=x.get('usage'))
406:                   for x in by('image')],
407:            pss_after_load_mib=mib(d.get('pss_after_load')),
408:            peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
409:            pss_image_before_after_mib=[mib(d.get('pss_before_image')), mib(d.get('pss_after_image'))],
410:            checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
411:                        cached_reuse=bool(by('cached')) and all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
412:                        replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r)),
413:            min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
414:            lmk=d.get('lmk'), server_exit=d.get('server_exit'), exit_clean=d.get('exit_clean'),
415:            skin=[m.get('skin_start'), m.get('skin_end')], status_max=m.get('status_max'),
416:            caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()}, power=m.get('power')))
417:    for line in lines:
418:        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
419:    # VALID covers execution conditions only; these say whether blocks are comparable at all.
420:    timed = [x for x in lines if not x['block'].endswith('M') and x['label'] == 'VALID']
421:    print(json.dumps(dict(comparability=dict(
422:        gen_tokens_identical=len({tuple(x['gen_tokens']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
423:        uncached_prompt_n_identical=len({tuple(x['uncached_prompt_n']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
424:        all_checks_pass=all(all(x['checks'].values()) for x in lines)))), flush=True)
425:    return lines

427:
428:def main(argv=None):
429:    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
430:    ap.add_argument('--blocks', default='A,B,B,A')
431:    ap.add_argument('--smoke', action='store_true')
432:    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
433:    ap.add_argument('--summarize', type=Path)
434:    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
435:    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
436:    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
437:    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
438:    if a.preflight:
439:        return preflight()
440:    if a.monitor:
441:        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
442:    if a.summarize:
443:        return summarize(a.summarize)
444:    selected = blocks(a.blocks)
445:    if a.dry_run:
446:        gen, unc, cached = payloads()
447:        print(DRY_LABEL)
448:        print(json.dumps(dict(blocks=selected, commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
449:                              sequence=[(b, [(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, {})])
450:                                        for b in sorted(set(selected))],
451:                              uncached_chars=len(unc[1]['prompt'])), indent=1))
452:        return
453:    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
454:        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis2/oneshot.sh into Debian')
455:    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
456:    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
457:        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
458:    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
459:        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
460:    v1.free_ports()
461:    telemetry(bridge)
462:    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
463:    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
464:    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
465:        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
466:    gen, unc, cached = payloads()
467:    image = image_payload() if 'M' in selected else None
468:    if any(v1.sha(p) != h for p, h in WEIGHTS.items()):
469:        raise SystemExit('model/projector/drafter SHA-256 mismatch')
470:    idle = None if a.smoke else json.loads((bridge/'idle.json').read_text())['skin']
471:    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=manifest(selected, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), idle_skin=idle,
472:             note='Short blocks; thermal equivalence between blocks is not established (#93). '
473:                  + (SMOKE_LABEL if a.smoke else 'ABBA order controls linear drift only.')))
474:    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
475:    for i, b in enumerate(selected):
476:        result = run_block(b, i, out, bridge, idle, sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), a.smoke)
477:        print(f"{i:02d} {b}: {result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
478:        if result['label'] == 'NOT VALID':
479:            raise SystemExit('stopped; later blocks not run. See README recovery.')
480:    summarize(out)
481:    print('Finished. Package RUN_FOLDER (see README); do not rerun for analysis.', flush=True)
482:
483:
484:if __name__ == '__main__':
485:    try:
486:        main()
487:    finally:
488:        for proc in v1.OWNED:
489:            v1.own_stop(proc)

review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:46:
review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:47:   A successful acknowledgement resolves the original race: the monitor samples sequentially, so a completed sample begun after clearing the target also establishes that the earlier sample finished. However, shutdown proceeds when acknowledgement times out.
review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:48:
review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:49:   Concrete scenario: a native sample stalls for more than five seconds before reading the cold server’s `/proc` files. Detachment returns false, shutdown removes those files, and the delayed sample records an error. The warm gate or final sampling-error check can then reject otherwise successful work. This remains fail-closed for measurement validity, so it is nonblocking.
review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:50:
review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:51:   Make acknowledgement failure stop the block explicitly after owned-server cleanup, and describe the bounded fallback accurately. A delayed-sample lifecycle test would cover this better than the current synthetic future-timestamp test.
review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:52:
review_inputs/approved_round2_review.txt:53:The other round 1 findings are addressed: relevant environment overrides are rejected before server launch; pooling compares commands; the warm phase receives its own thermal gate and starting-temperature summary; foreign mapped libraries immediately reject the block; and Debian preflight checks for `requests`.

101:def get(path):
102:    with urllib.request.urlopen('http://127.0.0.1:8091'+path, timeout=10) as r:
103:        return json.load(r)

106:def verify_config(block):
107:    """The running server must report the intended projector and MTP state; never adjust flags."""
108:    c = config_of(block)
109:    props, slots = get('/props'), get('/slots')
110:    vision = props.get('modalities', {}).get('vision')
111:    spec = [s.get('speculative') for s in slots]
112:    got = dict(vision=vision, speculative=spec)
113:    if vision is not c['q8'] or spec != [c['mtp']]:
114:        raise RuntimeError(f'configuration mismatch: expected q8={c["q8"]} mtp={c["mtp"]}, server reports {got}')
115:    return got

118:def fresh_pss(bridge, pid, wait=5):
119:    """PSS of this server from a monitor sample begun after now, taken while the server is idle."""
120:    after, deadline = time.monotonic(), time.monotonic() + wait
121:    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
122:        for s in reversed(v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')):
123:            if s.get('t_start', -1) <= after:
124:                break
125:            if s.get('pid') == pid:
126:                return dict(pss_kib=s.get('pss_kib'), t=s['t'], after=after)
127:        time.sleep(.2)
128:    return dict(pss_kib=None, t=None, after=after, unavailable=f'no fresh sample within {wait} s')

131:def telemetry(bridge):
132:    """Marked copy of vis1_run.telemetry (VIS2-BATTERY-FIX): no 80 % upper bound; all other stops kept."""
133:    if (bridge/'abort').exists():
134:        raise SystemExit('owner aborted wrapper')
135:    d = json.loads((bridge/'latest.json').read_text())
136:    if time.monotonic() - d['t'] > 12:
137:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry stale')
138:    if d.get('error'):
139:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry failed: ' + d['error'])
140:    if d['capacity'] < 50 or d['battery_status'] != 'Discharging':
141:        raise RuntimeError('battery below 50% or not Discharging')
142:    if d['agent']:
143:        v1.log_agent_matches(d.get('agent_detail', []))
144:        raise RuntimeError('agent found')
145:    if d['skin'] is None or d['status'] is None:
146:        raise RuntimeError('missing VIRTUAL-SKIN or Android status')
147:    if d['status'] >= 4 or d['bat_c'] >= 45:
148:        raise RuntimeError('thermal stop (Android CRITICAL or battery >=45 C)')
149:    return d

152:def gate(bridge, idle):
153:    """Marked copy of vis1_run.gate using the local telemetry()."""
154:    start = time.monotonic()
155:    while True:
156:        d = telemetry(bridge)
157:        if v1.gate_ok(d['skin'], idle):
158:            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=time.monotonic()-start, missed=False)
159:        if time.monotonic()-start >= 900:
160:            raise RuntimeError('gate missed: not cool after 15 minutes; no warm-start block')
161:        time.sleep(5)

163:
164:def guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, timeout):
165:    """Request in a worker; telemetry/abort/affinity stay enforced every 0.5 s while it runs.
166:    A violation raises here and run_block's finally stops the owned server, ending the request."""
167:    result = {}
168:
169:    def work():
170:        try:
171:            result['data'] = v1.post(endpoint, body, timeout=timeout)
172:        except BaseException as e:
173:            result['error'] = e
174:    th = threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True)
175:    th.start()
176:    while th.is_alive():
177:        th.join(.5)
178:        if th.is_alive():
179:            telemetry(bridge)
180:            v1.check_cores(pid)
181:    if 'error' in result:
182:        raise result['error']
183:    return result['data']

185:
186:def sample_violations(samples):
187:    """Every recorded sample must satisfy the same stops telemetry() enforces live."""
188:    bad = []
189:    if any(s.get('agent') for s in samples):
190:        bad.append('agent')
191:    if any(s.get('battery_status') != 'Discharging' or s.get('capacity', -1) < 50 for s in samples):
192:        bad.append('battery band')
193:    if any(s.get('cores_ok') is False for s in samples):
194:        bad.append('affinity')
195:    if any((s.get('status') or 0) >= 4 or (s.get('bat_c') or 0) >= 45 for s in samples):
196:        bad.append('thermal stop threshold')
197:    return bad

200:def run_block(block, index, out, bridge, idle, seq, smoke):
201:    record = dict(phase_start=time.monotonic(), block=block, index=index, config=config_of(block),
202:                  label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'NOT VALID', reason='interrupted', requests=[],
203:                  command=command(block), started_epoch=time.time(), smoke=smoke)
204:    attempt = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ')
205:    path = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.json'
206:    logpath = out/f'{index:02d}_{block}_{attempt}.server.log'
207:    proc = pid = None
208:    try:
209:        if not smoke:
210:            record['gate'] = gate(bridge, idle)
211:        v1.free_ports()
212:        if v1.agent_found(log=True):
213:            raise RuntimeError('agent found before launch')
214:        if not smoke:  # cold load: same native cache-drop handshake as VIS-1
215:            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink(missing_ok=True)
216:            (bridge/'drop_request').touch()
217:            began = time.monotonic()
218:            while not (bridge/'drop_done').exists():
219:                if time.monotonic()-began > 30:
220:                    raise RuntimeError('cache-drop handshake timeout')
221:                time.sleep(.1)
222:            if (bridge/'drop_done').read_text().strip() != 'ok':
223:                raise RuntimeError('cache drop failed')
224:            (bridge/'drop_request').unlink()
225:            (bridge/'drop_done').unlink()
226:            record['gate_after_drop'] = gate(bridge, idle)
227:        d = telemetry(bridge)
228:        record['battery_start'] = dict(capacity=d['capacity'], voltage_now_uV=d['voltage_now_uV'], t=d['t'])
229:        with logpath.open('w') as log:
230:            began = time.monotonic()
231:            proc = v1.spawn(record['command'], log)
232:            while pid is None:
233:                if proc.poll() is not None or time.monotonic()-began > 10:
234:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed or owned child not found')
235:                try:
236:                    pid = v1.server_pid(proc)
237:                except (OSError, RuntimeError):
238:                    time.sleep(.05)
239:            v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=pid))
240:            while True:
241:                if proc.poll() is not None:
242:                    raise RuntimeError('server crashed during load')
243:                try:
244:                    if v1.post('/health', timeout=1).get('status') == 'ok':
245:                        break
246:                except (OSError, ValueError):
247:                    pass
248:                telemetry(bridge)
249:                if time.monotonic()-began >= 180:
250:                    raise RuntimeError('server load timeout')
251:                time.sleep(.2)
252:            record.update(server_pid=pid, load_s=time.monotonic()-began, loaded_at=time.monotonic())
253:            v1.check_cores(pid)
254:            record['reported'] = verify_config(block)
255:            record['pss_after_load'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
256:            warm = next(x for x in seq if x[0] == 'gen')
257:            guarded_post(bridge, pid, warm[2], warm[3], REQ_TIMEOUT_S)  # discarded warm-up (generation)
258:            record['measure_start'] = start = time.monotonic()
259:            for kind, name, endpoint, body in seq:
260:                telemetry(bridge)
261:                v1.check_cores(pid)
262:                left = BLOCK_CAP_S - (time.monotonic()-start)
263:                if left <= 0:
264:                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
265:                if kind == 'image':
266:                    record['pss_before_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
267:                offset = logpath.stat().st_size
268:                t = time.monotonic()
269:                data = guarded_post(bridge, pid, endpoint, body, min(REQ_TIMEOUT_S, left))
270:                end = time.monotonic()
271:                if end-start > BLOCK_CAP_S:
272:                    raise RuntimeError(f'block cap {BLOCK_CAP_S} s exceeded')
273:                if kind == 'image':
274:                    record['pss_after_image'] = fresh_pss(bridge, pid)
275:                with logpath.open() as f:
276:                    f.seek(offset)
277:                    delta = f.read()
278:                row = v1.request_record(name, data, t, end, delta, 'V2' if kind == 'image' else 'T2')
279:                row.update(kind=kind, cache_n=row['timings'].get('cache_n'),
280:                           prompt_per_second=row['timings'].get('prompt_per_second'))
281:                record['requests'].append(row)
282:                v1.write(path, record)
283:            record['measure_end'] = time.monotonic()
284:            if proc.poll() is not None:
285:                raise RuntimeError('server crashed during measurement')
286:            v1.check_cores(pid)
287:            telemetry(bridge)
288:            record.update(label=SMOKE_LABEL if smoke else 'VALID', reason='completed fixed request sequence')
289:    except (Exception, SystemExit) as e:
290:        record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason=str(e))
291:    finally:
292:        record['stop_start'] = time.monotonic()
293:        v1.write(bridge/'target.json', dict(pid=None))
294:        if proc is not None:
295:            record['server_exit'] = v1.own_stop(proc, pid)
296:            record['exit_clean'] = record['server_exit'] == 0 and not v1.group_alive(proc.pid)
297:        record['stopped'] = time.monotonic()
298:        record['samples'] = [s for s in v1.rows(bridge/'samples.jsonl')
299:                             if record['phase_start']-1 <= s['t'] <= record['stopped']]
300:        record['lmk'] = v1.lmk_request(bridge, record['started_epoch'])
301:        record['metrics'] = v1.metrics(record)
302:        if record['label'] != 'NOT VALID':  # VALID and SMOKE both need a clean finish
303:            bad = []
304:            if proc is not None and not record['exit_clean']:
305:                bad.append(f"server cleanup not clean (exit {record['server_exit']})")
306:            if not record['lmk'].get('ok'):
307:                bad.append('LMK query failed')
308:            if sample_violations(record['samples']):
309:                bad.append('sample violation: ' + ', '.join(sample_violations(record['samples'])))
310:            if [e for e in v1.rows(bridge/'errors.jsonl') if record['phase_start'] <= e['t'] <= record['stop_start']]:
311:                bad.append('sampling failed')
312:            if bad:
313:                record.update(label='NOT VALID', reason='; '.join(bad))
314:        v1.write(path, record)
315:    return record

332:def manifest(selected, smoke, gen, unc, cached, image):
333:    allp = gen + [unc] + cached + ([(IMAGE_FRAME, image)] if image else [])
334:    return dict(blocks=selected, smoke=smoke, rounds=ROUNDS, gen_n=GEN_N, block_cap_s=BLOCK_CAP_S,
335:                commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
336:                payload_sha256={n: hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(p, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest() for n, p in allp},
337:                code_sha256={p: v1.sha(REPO/p) for p in code_paths()},
338:                weight_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in WEIGHTS},
339:                binary_sha256={str(p): v1.sha(p) for p in [BIN] + LIBS},
340:                base_commit=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd=REPO, text=True).strip(),
341:                git_status=subprocess.check_output(['git', 'status', '--porcelain=v1'], cwd=REPO, text=True))

344:def preflight():
345:    """Before and after the 5-minute idle: ports, agents, files/hashes, plus Debian imports or native root/battery/cpuset."""
346:    v1.free_ports()
347:    matches = v1.agent_matches()
348:    if matches:
349:        v1.log_agent_matches(matches)
350:        raise SystemExit('agent found; close Claude/AGY/Codex (see pid/comm/cmdline above)')
351:    missing = [str(p) for p in (*WEIGHTS, BIN) if not p.is_file()]
352:    if missing or not LIBS:
353:        raise SystemExit('missing file(s): ' + ', '.join(missing or ['llama.cpp-vis shared libraries']))
354:    bad = [str(p) for p, h in WEIGHTS.items() if v1.sha(p) != h]
355:    if bad:
356:        raise SystemExit('SHA-256 mismatch: ' + ', '.join(bad))
357:    if Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
358:        missing_mods = [m for m in v1.DEBIAN_IMPORTS if importlib.util.find_spec(m) is None]
359:        if missing_mods:
360:            raise SystemExit('Debian python3 is missing ' + ', '.join(missing_mods))
361:    else:
362:        if subprocess.run(['su', '-c', 'id'], capture_output=True, timeout=10).returncode != 0:
363:            raise SystemExit('no root (su) access; telemetry needs root')
364:        cap, status = v1.root(f'cat {v1.BAT}/capacity {v1.BAT}/status', 'preflight_battery').split()[:2]
365:        if not (cap.isdigit() and int(cap) >= 50 and status == 'Discharging'):
366:            raise SystemExit('battery below 50% or not Discharging; unplug the charger')
367:        before = os.sched_getaffinity(0)
368:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
369:        stuck = os.sched_getaffinity(0) == {4, 5, 6, 7}
370:        os.sched_setaffinity(0, before)
371:        if not stuck:
372:            raise SystemExit('Termux cpuset blocks cores 4-7; bring Termux to the foreground, screen on')
373:    print('PREFLIGHT OK', flush=True)

376:def mib(snap):
377:    return snap['pss_kib'] and round(snap['pss_kib']/1024) if snap else None

380:def med(xs):
381:    xs = [x for x in xs if x is not None]
382:    return round(statistics.median(xs), 2) if xs else None

385:def summarize(folder):
386:    """Per-block medians from saved records only; missing fields print as None (unavailable)."""
387:    lines = []
388:    for p in sorted(Path(folder).glob('[0-9][0-9]_[A-DM]_*Z.json')):
389:        d = json.loads(p.read_text())
390:        r, m = d['requests'], d.get('metrics', {})
391:        by = lambda k: [x for x in r if x['kind'] == k]
392:        dn = sum(x.get('draft_n') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
393:        da = sum(x.get('draft_n_accepted') or 0 for x in by('gen'))
394:        lines.append(dict(
395:            block=f"{d['index']:02d}_{d['block']}", label=d['label'], reason=d['reason'], load_s=med([d.get('load_s')]),
396:            battery_start=d.get('battery_start'),
397:            reported=d.get('reported'), uncached_pp_tok_s=med([x['prompt_per_second'] for x in by('uncached')]),
398:            uncached_prompt_n=sorted({x['prompt_tokens'] for x in by('uncached')}),
399:            cached_ms=med([x['prompt_ms'] for x in by('cached')]),
400:            cached_cache_n=sorted({x['cache_n'] for x in by('cached')}),
401:            gen_tok_s=med([x['predicted_per_second'] for x in by('gen')]),
402:            gen_tokens=[x['generated_tokens'] for x in by('gen')],
403:            gen_wall_ms=med([x['wall_ms'] for x in by('gen')]),
404:            draft_acceptance=round(da/dn, 3) if dn else None,
405:            image=[dict(wall_ms=round(x['wall_ms']), prompt_tokens=x['prompt_tokens'], usage=x.get('usage'))
406:                   for x in by('image')],
407:            pss_after_load_mib=mib(d.get('pss_after_load')),
408:            peak_pss_mib=m.get('peak_pss_kib') and round(m['peak_pss_kib']/1024),
409:            pss_image_before_after_mib=[mib(d.get('pss_before_image')), mib(d.get('pss_after_image'))],
410:            checks=dict(uncached_cache_n_zero=all(x['cache_n'] == 0 for x in by('uncached')),
411:                        cached_reuse=bool(by('cached')) and all((x['cache_n'] or 0) > 0 for x in by('cached')),
412:                        replies_nonempty=all((x.get('reply') or '').strip() for x in r)),
413:            min_mem_available_mib=m.get('min_mem_available_kib') and round(m['min_mem_available_kib']/1024),
414:            lmk=d.get('lmk'), server_exit=d.get('server_exit'), exit_clean=d.get('exit_clean'),
415:            skin=[m.get('skin_start'), m.get('skin_end')], status_max=m.get('status_max'),
416:            caps={k: round(v['percent']) for k, v in m.get('caps', {}).items()}, power=m.get('power')))
417:    for line in lines:
418:        print(json.dumps(line), flush=True)
419:    # VALID covers execution conditions only; these say whether blocks are comparable at all.
420:    timed = [x for x in lines if not x['block'].endswith('M') and x['label'] == 'VALID']
421:    print(json.dumps(dict(comparability=dict(
422:        gen_tokens_identical=len({tuple(x['gen_tokens']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
423:        uncached_prompt_n_identical=len({tuple(x['uncached_prompt_n']) for x in timed}) <= 1,
424:        all_checks_pass=all(all(x['checks'].values()) for x in lines)))), flush=True)
425:    return lines

427:
428:def main(argv=None):
429:    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
430:    ap.add_argument('--blocks', default='A,B,B,A')
431:    ap.add_argument('--smoke', action='store_true')
432:    ap.add_argument('--dry-run', action='store_true')
433:    ap.add_argument('--summarize', type=Path)
434:    ap.add_argument('--bridge', type=Path)
435:    ap.add_argument('--preflight', action='store_true', help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
436:    ap.add_argument('--monitor', type=Path, help=argparse.SUPPRESS)
437:    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
438:    if a.preflight:
439:        return preflight()
440:    if a.monitor:
441:        return v1.monitor(a.monitor)
442:    if a.summarize:
443:        return summarize(a.summarize)
444:    selected = blocks(a.blocks)
445:    if a.dry_run:
446:        gen, unc, cached = payloads()
447:        print(DRY_LABEL)
448:        print(json.dumps(dict(blocks=selected, commands={b: command(b) for b in sorted(set(selected))},
449:                              sequence=[(b, [(k, n, e) for k, n, e, _ in sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, {})])
450:                                        for b in sorted(set(selected))],
451:                              uncached_chars=len(unc[1]['prompt'])), indent=1))
452:        return
453:    if a.bridge is None or not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
454:        raise SystemExit('launch only through native vis2/oneshot.sh into Debian')
455:    out = bridge = a.bridge.resolve()
456:    if not (bridge/'owner_launch').is_file():
457:        raise SystemExit('missing owner wrapper receipt')
458:    if v1.agent_found(log=True):
459:        raise SystemExit('agent found; no server started')
460:    v1.free_ports()
461:    telemetry(bridge)
462:    os.sched_setaffinity(0, {4, 5, 6, 7})
463:    v1.check_cores(os.getpid())
464:    for sig in (signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGHUP):
465:        signal.signal(sig, v1.interrupted)
466:    gen, unc, cached = payloads()
467:    image = image_payload() if 'M' in selected else None
468:    if any(v1.sha(p) != h for p, h in WEIGHTS.items()):
469:        raise SystemExit('model/projector/drafter SHA-256 mismatch')
470:    idle = None if a.smoke else json.loads((bridge/'idle.json').read_text())['skin']
471:    v1.write(out/'run.json', dict(manifest=manifest(selected, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), idle_skin=idle,
472:             note='Short blocks; thermal equivalence between blocks is not established (#93). '
473:                  + (SMOKE_LABEL if a.smoke else 'ABBA order controls linear drift only.')))
474:    print('RUN_FOLDER='+str(out), flush=True)
475:    for i, b in enumerate(selected):
476:        result = run_block(b, i, out, bridge, idle, sequence(b, a.smoke, gen, unc, cached, image), a.smoke)
477:        print(f"{i:02d} {b}: {result['label']} — {result['reason']}", flush=True)
478:        if result['label'] == 'NOT VALID':
479:            raise SystemExit('stopped; later blocks not run. See README recovery.')
480:    summarize(out)
481:    print('Finished. Package RUN_FOLDER (see README); do not rerun for analysis.', flush=True)
482:
483:
484:if __name__ == '__main__':
485:    try:
486:        main()
487:    finally:
488:        for proc in v1.OWNED:
489:            v1.own_stop(proc)

20:@contextlib.contextmanager
21:def patched(*triples):
22:    saved = [(obj, name, getattr(obj, name)) for obj, name, _ in triples]
23:    try:
24:        for obj, name, value in triples:
25:            setattr(obj, name, value)
26:        yield
27:    finally:
28:        for obj, name, value in saved:
29:            setattr(obj, name, value)

32:def deployed_command(setup):
33:    """The exact shell command server_manager.start_setup builds; os.system and waits are stubbed."""
34:    sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO))
35:    import server_manager as sm
36:    calls = []
37:    with patched((sm.os, 'system', calls.append), (sm.time, 'sleep', lambda s: None),
38:                 (sm, 'wait_for_server', lambda port=8080, timeout=60: True), (sm, 'mtp_draft_ok', lambda: True)):
39:        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
40:            sm.start_setup(setup)
41:    return shlex.split(calls[-1].replace(' 2>/dev/null &', ''))

262:def test_preflight_refusals():
263:    cfg = v.config('gen', 'new')
264:    spec = dict(weight_sha256={str(p): h for p, h in v.WEIGHTS.items()})
265:    real = v.importlib.util.find_spec
266:    base = ((v1, 'free_ports', lambda: None), (v1, 'agent_matches', lambda: []), (v, 'check_env', lambda: None),
267:            (v, 'manifest', lambda c: spec))
268:    with patched(*base, (v.importlib.util, 'find_spec', lambda m: None if m == 'requests' else real(m))):
269:        try:
270:            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as out:
271:                v.preflight(cfg)
272:        except SystemExit as e:
273:            assert 'requests' in str(e) and 'PREFLIGHT OK' not in out.getvalue()
274:        else:
275:            raise AssertionError('preflight must refuse without requests')
276:    with patched(*base, (v, 'done_before', lambda s: Path('runs/prior'))):
277:        try:
278:            v.preflight(cfg)
279:        except SystemExit as e:
280:            assert 'already measured' in str(e)
281:        else:
282:            raise AssertionError('preflight must refuse a repeated VALID test')
283:        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()) as out:
284:            v.preflight(cfg, repeat=True)
285:        assert 'PREFLIGHT OK' in out.getvalue()

158:def own_stop(proc, server=None):
159:    """Only the private process group created by our Popen; timeout is a second hard bound."""
160:    if proc.poll() is None:
161:        # VIS1-FIX3: GNU timeout re-sends a TERM it receives to its whole group (kill(0, sig)), so
162:        # TERM to timeout reached llama-server twice ("second interrupt" -> abort). TERM the owned
163:        # server once (still our direct child in our group); timeout then exits with its status.
164:        # Server not known yet: fall back to timeout.
165:        owned = server is not None and (server, proc.pid, proc.pid) in set(proc_rows())
166:        try:
167:            os.kill(server if owned else proc.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
168:            proc.wait(timeout=3)
169:        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
170:            os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
171:            proc.wait(timeout=5)
172:        except ProcessLookupError:
173:            proc.wait(timeout=5)
174:    return proc.returncode

177:OWNED = []
178:DEFERRED = dict(on=False, signum=None)
179:
180:
181:def interrupted(n, frame):
182:    if DEFERRED['on']:
183:        DEFERRED['signum'] = n
184:    else:
185:        raise SystemExit(f'interrupted {n}')
186:
187:
188:def spawn(cmd, log):
189:    DEFERRED.update(on=True, signum=None)
190:    try:
191:        proc = subprocess.Popen(cmd, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=log, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
192:                                start_new_session=True)
193:        OWNED.append(proc)
194:    finally:
195:        DEFERRED['on'] = False
196:    if DEFERRED['signum'] is not None:
197:        own_stop(proc)
198:        raise SystemExit(f'interrupted {DEFERRED["signum"]}')
199:    return proc

202:AGENT_NAMES = {'claude', 'codex', 'agy'}
203:
204:
205:def agent_matches():
206:    """Processes whose own binary is claude/codex/agy, not merely a path or argument containing those
207:    letters (VIS1-FIX: pgrep -f alone matches substrings anywhere in argv, e.g. a --config path under
208:    ~/.codex/). pgrep -f still casts the wide net; comm/argv0 narrows it to real agent processes,
209:    including a lingering app-server-daemon left behind after the owner closes the interactive CLI."""
210:    p = subprocess.run(['pgrep', '-f', 'claude|codex|agy'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
211:    if p.returncode not in (0, 1):
212:        raise RuntimeError('agent process check failed')
213:    found = []
214:    for token in p.stdout.split():
215:        pid = int(token)
216:        if pid == os.getpid():
217:            continue
218:        try:
219:            comm = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/comm').read_text().strip()
220:            parts = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/cmdline').read_bytes().split(b'\0')
221:            cmdline = ' '.join(c.decode(errors='replace') for c in parts if c)
222:        except (FileNotFoundError, ProcessLookupError):
223:            continue  # exited between pgrep and read
224:        argv0 = Path(cmdline.split(' ', 1)[0]).name if cmdline else ''
225:        if comm in AGENT_NAMES or argv0 in AGENT_NAMES:
226:            found.append(dict(pid=pid, comm=comm, cmdline=cmdline))
227:    return found
228:

230:def log_agent_matches(matches):
231:    for m in matches:
232:        print(f"agent match: pid={m['pid']} comm={m['comm']} cmdline={m['cmdline']}", flush=True)
233:

235:def agent_found(log=False):
236:    matches = agent_matches()
237:    if matches and log:
238:        log_agent_matches(matches)
239:    return bool(matches)

242:def free_ports():
243:    if os.environ.get('PREFIX') == '/data/data/com.termux/files/usr' and not Path('/termux-home').is_dir():
244:        listeners = root('/system/bin/ss -H -ltn','ports')
245:        for line in listeners.splitlines():
246:            fields = line.split()
247:            if len(fields) >= 4 and re.search(r':(?:8080|8090|8091)$',fields[3]):
248:                raise RuntimeError('existing listener on a VIS-1 reserved port')
249:    for port in (8080, 8090, 8091):
250:        # Check IPv4 and IPv6 loopback, including non-HTTP listeners.
251:        for family, host in ((socket.AF_INET, '127.0.0.1'), (socket.AF_INET6, '::1')):
252:            with socket.socket(family) as s:
253:                s.settimeout(.3)
254:                if s.connect_ex((host, port)) == 0:
255:                    raise RuntimeError(f'port {port} already has a listener')

257:
258:def gate_ok(skin, idle):
259:    return skin is not None and math.isfinite(skin) and skin <= idle + 1.5
260:
261:
262:def telemetry(bridge, allow_agent=False):
263:    if (bridge/'abort').exists():
264:        raise SystemExit('owner aborted wrapper')
265:    d = json.loads((bridge/'latest.json').read_text())
266:    if time.monotonic() - d['t'] > 12:
267:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry stale')
268:    if d.get('error'):
269:        raise RuntimeError('native telemetry failed: ' + d['error'])
270:    if not 50 <= d['capacity'] <= 80 or d['battery_status'] != 'Discharging':
271:        raise RuntimeError('battery outside 50-80% Discharging band')
272:    if d['agent'] and not allow_agent:
273:        log_agent_matches(d.get('agent_detail', []))
274:        raise RuntimeError('agent found')
275:    if d['skin'] is None or d['status'] is None:
276:        raise RuntimeError('missing VIRTUAL-SKIN or Android status')
277:    if d['status'] >= 4 or d['bat_c'] >= 45:
278:        raise RuntimeError('thermal stop (Android CRITICAL or battery >=45 C)')
279:    return d
280:
281:
282:def gate(bridge, idle, now=time.monotonic, sleep=time.sleep):
283:    start = now()
284:    while True:
285:        d = telemetry(bridge)
286:        if gate_ok(d['skin'], idle):
287:            return dict(skin=d['skin'], status=d['status'], waited_s=now()-start, missed=False)
288:        if now()-start >= 900:
289:            raise RuntimeError('gate missed: not cool after 15 minutes; no warm-start block')
290:        sleep(5)

292:
293:def post(path, body=None, timeout=30):
294:    req = urllib.request.Request('http://127.0.0.1:8091'+path,
295:        data=None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode(),
296:        headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
297:    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
298:        return json.load(r)

300:
301:def proc_rows():
302:    """(pid, ppid, pgid) for every process, from /proc/<n>/stat (comm may contain spaces/parens)."""
303:    for d in Path('/proc').iterdir():
304:        if d.name.isdigit():
305:            try:
306:                rest = (d/'stat').read_text().rsplit(')', 1)[1].split()
307:                yield int(d.name), int(rest[1]), int(rest[2])
308:            except (OSError, IndexError, ValueError):
309:                pass  # exited mid-scan

311:
312:def server_pid(proc):
313:    # VIS1-FIX2: /proc/<pid>/task/<pid>/children does not exist on this kernel, so every block
314:    # timed out here and killed a healthy server. Same rule as before, via ppid/pgid, never by name.
315:    children = [pid for pid, ppid, pgid in proc_rows() if ppid == proc.pid and pgid == proc.pid]
316:    if len(children) != 1:
317:        raise RuntimeError('cannot identify the owned timeout child')
318:    return children[0]
319:
320:
321:def group_alive(pgid):
322:    return any(g == pgid for _, _, g in proc_rows())
323:
324:
325:def check_cores(pid):
326:    # Also check the Android cpuset: affinity alone can hide a background restriction.
327:    status = Path(f'/proc/{pid}/status').read_text()
328:    m = re.search(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$', status, re.M)
329:    if not m or m.group(1).strip() != '4-7':
330:        raise RuntimeError('Termux/server moved off cores 4-7')
331:    # Each thread inherits the launch mask; inspect every server thread for later changes.
332:    for p in Path(f'/proc/{pid}/task').glob('*/status'):
333:        match = re.search(r'^Cpus_allowed_list:\s*(.+)$', p.read_text(), re.M)
334:        if not match or match.group(1).strip() != '4-7':
335:            raise RuntimeError('server thread moved off cores 4-7')

338:VIS0_PARSER = None
339:
340:def request_record(name, data, start, end, log_delta, block):
341:    tm = data.get('timings', {})
342:    if not tm:
343:        raise RuntimeError('response missing server timings')
344:    reply = data.get('content')
345:    if block.startswith('V'):
346:        reply = data['choices'][0]['message']['content']
347:    # VIS-0 log parser has no server side effects. Rename its agent-era field explicitly.
348:    global VIS0_PARSER
349:    if block.startswith('V') and VIS0_PARSER is None:
350:        VIS0_PARSER = module('vis1_vis0', 'benchmark/gemma_vision/vis0/vis0.py').parse_server_log_delta
351:    parsed = VIS0_PARSER(log_delta) if block.startswith('V') else {}
352:    return dict(input=name, started=start, ended=end, wall_ms=(end-start)*1000, reply=reply,
353:                timings=tm, prompt_tokens=tm.get('prompt_n'), prompt_ms=tm.get('prompt_ms'),
354:                generated_tokens=tm.get('predicted_n'), predicted_per_second=tm.get('predicted_per_second'),
355:                draft_n=tm.get('draft_n'), draft_n_accepted=tm.get('draft_n_accepted'),
356:                image_encode_ms=parsed.get('image_encode_ms_not_a_measurement'),
357:                image_tokens=parsed.get('image_tokens_logged'), usage=data.get('usage'),
358:                budget=None if block.startswith('T') else (70 if block in ('V1','V2') else 140),
359:                mmproj=None if block.startswith('T') else ('BF16' if block in ('V1','V4') else 'Q8_0'))
